{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"bets","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Dylan owes Fletcher $30 from an unsuccessful bet.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A wager, an agreement between two parties that a stake (usually money) will be paid by the loser to the winner (the winner being the one who correctly forecast the outcome of an event)."]},{"glosses":["A candidate (for elections and pageants) or competitor (in multinational sports)."],"tags":["Philippines","figuratively","informal"]},{"examples":[{"text":"It’s a safe bet that it will rain tomorrow.","type":"example"},{"text":"There's a decent bet that we'll be able to reach the top of that hill in an hour.","type":"example"},{"text":"It’s an even bet that Jim will come top of the maths test tomorrow.","type":"example"},{"text":"There's a good bet that Sally will arrive later.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Indicating a degree of certainty, or that something can be relied upon."]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"bĕt"},{"ipa":"/bɛt/"}],"upstream_line":29994,"upstream_offset":200888384,"upstream_record_sha256":"909fe85c62e27e8f41fe02f9c5b5b342dd4fb5cde9b369a8a107a8e3c5a3c62a","word":"bet"}
